are defined similarly to (
6.262
) by the recursive formulas,

\displaystyle\mathscr{D}_{m+1}=\mathscr{D}_{m}+\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\rfloor\quad\mbox{ and }\quad\mathscr{R}_{m+1}=\Phi_{m}^{-1}\left(-\Psi_{m}\,\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\rfloor+P_{N_{m}}^{\perp}\mathscr{R}_{m}+\mathscr{R}_{m}\Psi_{m}\right).

(6.277)

Remark that
\mathscr{D}_{m}
and
\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\rfloor
are Fourier multiplier Toeplitz operators that can be identified to their spectra
({\rm i}\mu_{j}^{m})_{j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}}
and
({\rm i}r_{j}^{m})_{j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}}
, namely

\displaystyle\forall(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c},\quad\mathscr{D}_{m}{\bf e}_{l,j}={\rm i}\mu_{j}^{m}\,{\bf e}_{l,j}\quad\hbox{and}\quad\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\rfloor\mathbf{e}_{l,j}={\rm i}r_{j}^{m}\,{\bf e}_{l,j}.

(6.278)

By construction, we find

\displaystyle\mu_{j}^{m+1}=\mu_{j}^{m}+r_{j}^{m}.

(6.279)

In a similar way to (
6.259
) we obtain

(\Psi_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\frac{-r_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)}{\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{0}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{0}(\lambda,\omega)}&\mbox{if }(l,j)\neq(0,j_{0})\\
0&\mbox{if }(l,j)=(0,j_{0}),\end{array}\right.

(6.280)

where the collection
\{r_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)\}
describes the Fourier coefficients of
\mathscr{R}_{m}
, that is,

\mathscr{R}_{m}{\bf e}_{l_{0},j_{0}}={\rm i}\sum_{(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d+1}}{r_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l_{0}-l){\bf e}_{l,j}}.

Now we shall define the open Cantor set where the preceding formula is meaningful,

\mathscr{O}_{m+1}^{\gamma}=\bigcap_{\underset{(l,j)\neq(0,j_{0})}{\underset{|l|\leqslant N_{m}}{(l,j,j_{0})\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times(\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c})^{2}}}}\left\{(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathscr{O}_{m}^{\gamma}\quad\textnormal{s.t.}\quad|\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)|>\tfrac{\gamma\langle j-j_{0}\rangle}{\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{2}}}\right\}.

(6.281)

Similarly to (
6.260
) and (
6.261
) we can extend (
6.280
) as follows

(\Psi_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}-\frac{\chi\left((\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega))(\gamma|j-j_{0}|)^{-1}\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{2}}\right){r}_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)}{\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)}&\mbox{if }(l,j)\neq(0,j_{0})\\
0&\mbox{if }(l,j)=(0,j_{0}).\end{array}\right.

(6.282)

We point out that working with this extension for
\Psi_{m}
allows to extend both
\mathscr{D}_{m+1}
and the remainder
\mathscr{R}_{m+1}
provided that the operators
\mathscr{D}_{m}
and
\mathscr{R}_{m}
are defined in the whole range of parameters. Thus the operator defined by the right-hand side in (
6.276
) can be extended to the whole set
\mathcal{O}
and we denote this extension by
\mathscr{L}_{m+1}
, that is,

\displaystyle\big(\omega\cdot\partial_{\varphi}+\mathscr{D}_{m+1}+\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\big)\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}:=\mathscr{L}_{m+1}.

(6.283)

This enables to construct by induction the sequence of operators
\left(\mathscr{L}_{m+1}\right)
in the full set
\mathcal{O}
. Similarly the operator
\Phi_{m}^{-1}\mathscr{L}_{m}\Phi_{m}
admits an extension in
\mathcal{O}
induced by the extension of
\Phi_{m}^{\pm 1}
. However, by construction the identity
\mathscr{L}_{m+1}=\Phi_{m}^{-1}\mathscr{L}_{m}\Phi_{m}
in (
6.276
) occurs in the Cantor set
\mathscr{O}_{m+1}^{\gamma}
and may fail outside this set.
We define

\delta_{m}(s):=\gamma^{-1}\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

(6.284)

and we want to prove by induction in
m\in\mathbb{N}
that

\forall\,m\in\mathbb{N},\quad\forall s\in[s_{0},\overline{s}_{l}],\,\delta_{m}(s)\leqslant\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\quad\mbox{ and }\quad\delta_{m}(s_{h})\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\right)\delta_{0}(s_{h}),

(6.285)

with
\overline{s}_{l}
and
s_{h}
fixed by (
6.244
). Moreover, we should check the validity of the assumptions (
6.264
) and (
6.268
) for
\mathscr{D}_{m+1}
and
\mathscr{R}_{m+1}
.
Notice that by Sobolev embeddings, it is sufficient to prove the first inequality with
s=\overline{s}_{l}.
The property is obvious for
m=0
. Now, assume that the property (
6.285
) is true for
m\in\mathbb{N}
and let us check it at the next order.
We write

\displaystyle\Phi_{m}^{-1}=\hbox{Id}+\Sigma_{m}\quad\hbox{with}\quad\Sigma_{m}=\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{n}\Psi_{m}^{n}.

(6.286)

Thus similarly to (
6.3.2
), using in particular (
6.267
) and (
6.3.2
) we deduce successively

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(C\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\big)^{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle\delta_{m}(s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(C\delta_{m}(s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})\big)^{n}\right)

and for any
s\in[s_{0},S],

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(C\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\big)^{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s)\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(C\delta_{m}(s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})\big)^{n}\right).

Hence, from the induction assumption, the fact that
N_{m}\geqslant N_{0}
and since (
6.3
) implies in particular
s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}\leqslant\overline{s}_{l}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\big)^{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(C\delta_{0}(s_{h})\big)^{n}\right)

and for any
s\in[s_{0},S],

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s)\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\big)^{n}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s)\left(1+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(C\delta_{0}(s_{h})\big)^{n}\right).

It follows from the condition (
6.3.2
) that

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\quad\hbox{and}\quad\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s).

(6.287)

One also gets

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\delta_{m}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}).

(6.288)

From KAM step (
6.273
) and Sobolev embeddings, we infer

\delta_{m+1}(\overline{s}_{l})\leqslant N_{m}^{\overline{s}_{l}-s_{h}}\delta_{m}(s_{h})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\left(\delta_{m}(\overline{s}_{l})\right)^{2}.

Using the induction assumption (
6.285
) yields

\displaystyle\delta_{m+1}(\overline{s}_{l})

\displaystyle\leqslant N_{m}^{\overline{s}_{l}-s_{h}}\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\right)\delta_{0}(s_{h})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{0}^{2}(s_{h})N_{0}^{2\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-2\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant 2N_{m}^{\overline{s}_{l}-s_{h}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{0}^{2}(s_{h})N_{0}^{2\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-2\mu_{2}}.

At this level we need to select the parameters
\overline{s}_{l},s_{h}
and
\mu_{2}
in such a way

N_{m}^{\overline{s}_{l}-s_{h}}\leqslant\frac{1}{4}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\qquad\hbox{and}\qquad CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{2\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-2\mu_{2}}\leqslant\frac{1}{2}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}

(6.289)

leading to

\delta_{m+1}(\overline{s}_{l})\leqslant\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}.

The conditions (
6.244
) imply in particular

s_{h}\geqslant\frac{3}{2}\mu_{2}+\overline{s}_{l}+1\quad\textnormal{and}\quad\mu_{2}\geqslant 2(\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})+1.

Then, using (
5.82
), we conclude that the assumptions of (
6.289
) hold true provided that

4N_{0}^{-\mu_{2}}\leqslant 1\qquad\hbox{and}\qquad 2CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\leqslant 1,

(6.290)

which follow from (
6.3.2
), since the first condition
4N_{0}^{-\mu_{2}}\leqslant 1
is automatically satisfied because
N_{0}\geqslant 2
and
\mu_{2}\geqslant 2,
according to (
6.244
).
Therefore, under the assumptions (
6.244
) we get the first statement of the induction in (
6.285
). The next goal is to establish the second estimate in (
6.285
). By KAM step (
6.273
) combined with the induction assumptions (
6.285
) we deduce that

\displaystyle\delta_{m+1}(s_{h})

\displaystyle\leqslant\delta_{m}(s_{h})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s_{0})\delta_{m}(s_{h})

\displaystyle\leqslant\Big(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\Big)\delta_{0}(s_{h})\Big(1+CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\Big).

Thus if one has

\Big(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\Big)\Big(1+CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\Big)\leqslant 2-\tfrac{1}{m+2},

(6.291)

then we get

\delta_{m+1}(s_{h})\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+2}\right)\delta_{0}(s_{h}),

which ends the induction argument of (
6.285
). Remark that with the choice
\mu_{2}\geqslant 2(\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})
fixed in (
6.244
), the condition (
6.291
) is staisfied if

CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\tau_{2}q-\tau_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\leqslant\tfrac{1}{(2m+1)(m+2)}\cdot

(6.292)

Since
N_{0}\geqslant 2
we may find a constant
c_{0}>0
small enough such that

\forall\,m\in\mathbb{N},\quad c_{0}N_{m}^{-1}\leq\tfrac{1}{(2m+1)(m+2)}\cdot

Consequently, (
6.292
) is satisfied provided that

CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\tau_{2}q-\tau_{2}+1}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\leqslant c_{0}.

(6.293)

By virtue of the assumption (
4.2
) we get in particular

\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-1\geqslant 0.

(6.294)

Thus (
6.293
) is satisfied in view of (
6.3.2
). To conclude the induction proof of (
6.285
) it remains to check that the assumptions (
6.264
) and (
6.268
) are satisfied for
\mathscr{D}_{m+1}
and
\mathscr{R}_{m+1}
. First, the assumption (
6.268
) is a consequence of the first inequality of (
6.285
) applied at the order
m+1
with the regularity index
s=s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}\leqslant\overline{s}_{l}
supplemented with (
6.3.2
). Concerning the validity of (
6.264
) for
\mathscr{D}_{m+1}
, we combine (
6.278
) and (
6.279
) and (
6.257
), in order to find

\displaystyle\|\mu_{j}^{m+1}-\mu_{j}^{m}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle=\big\|\langle P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{l,j},\mathbf{e}_{l,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}\big\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

From the Topeplitz structure of
\mathscr{R}_{m}
we may write

\displaystyle\|\mu_{j}^{m+1}-\mu_{j}^{m}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle=\big\|\langle P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}\big\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

By a duality argument combined with Lemma
4.3
and (
6.284
) we infer

\displaystyle\|\mu_{j}^{m+1}-\mu_{j}^{m}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\,\,\langle j\rangle^{-s_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\|_{H^{s_{0}}}\,\,\langle j\rangle^{-s_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}=\gamma\delta_{m}(s_{0}).

(6.295)

Hence we deduce from (
6.285
), (
6.252
) and (
6.245
)

\displaystyle\|\mu_{j}^{m+1}-\mu_{j}^{m}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}.

(6.296)

As the assumption (
6.264
) is satisfied with
\mathscr{D}_{m}
, that is,

\displaystyle\forall\,(j,j_{0})\in(\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c})^{2},\quad\max_{|\alpha|\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket}\sup_{(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathcal{O}}\left|\partial_{\lambda,\omega}^{\alpha}\left(\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)\right)\right|\leqslant C\,|j-j_{0}|,

(6.297)

then we obtain by (
6.3.2
)

\displaystyle\forall\,(j,j_{0})\in(\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c})^{2},\quad\max_{|\alpha|\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket}\sup_{(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathcal{O}}\left|\partial_{\lambda,\omega}^{\alpha}\left(\mu_{j}^{m+1}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m+1}(\lambda,\omega)\right)\right|\leqslant C\big(1+\varepsilon\gamma^{-1-q}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\big)\,|j-j_{0}|.

Consequently, the convergence of the series
\sum N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}
gives the required assumption with the same constant
C
independently of
m
. This completes the induction principle. In what follows, we shall provide some estimates for
\Psi_{m}
that will be used later to study the string convergence.
Using (
6.267
) combined with Lemma
4.3
and
s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\leqslant\overline{s}_{l}
we find

\displaystyle\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma^{-1}\|P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,{s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\,\delta_{m}(\overline{s}_{l}).

(6.298)

Thus (
6.285
) and (
6.3.2
) yield

\displaystyle\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\,\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\,N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}.

(6.299)

Next, we discuss the persistence of higher regularity. Let
s\in[s_{0},S]
, then from (
6.273
), (
6.285
) and (
6.3.2
) and (
6.294
)

\displaystyle\delta_{m+1}(s)

\displaystyle\leqslant\delta_{m}(s)\Big(1+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s_{0})\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant\delta_{m}(s)\Big(1+CN_{0}^{{\mu}_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{2}}\delta_{0}(s_{h})\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant\delta_{m}(s)\big(1+CN_{m}^{-1}\big).

Combining this estimate with (
5.82
) and (
6.252
) yields

\displaystyle\forall\,s\geqslant s_{0},\,\forall\,m\in\mathbb{N},\quad\delta_{m}(s)

\displaystyle\leqslant\delta_{0}(s)\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\big(1+CN_{n}^{-1}\big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\delta_{0}(s)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\left(1+\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right).

(6.300)

Using (
6.267
) combined with Lemma
4.3
, applying in particular interpolation inequalities, leads to

\displaystyle\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma^{-1}\|P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\,\delta_{m}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\,\delta_{m}^{\overline{\theta}}(s_{0})\delta_{m}^{1-\overline{\theta}}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1).

(6.301)

with
\overline{\theta}=\frac{1}{s-s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}.
Inserting (
6.285
) and (
6.3.2
) into (
6.3.2
) and using (
6.3.2
) give

\displaystyle\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\,\delta_{0}^{\overline{\theta}}(s_{2})\delta_{0}^{1-\overline{\theta}}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1)N_{0}^{\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\,{\varepsilon_{0}}^{\overline{\theta}}\delta_{0}^{1-\overline{\theta}}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1)N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}.

(6.302)

We point out that one also finds from (
6.288
), the second inequality of (
6.3.2
) and (
6.3.2
) that

\displaystyle\forall\,s\in[s_{0},S],\quad\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\left(\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\,C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\left(1+\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right).

(6.303)

\blacktriangleright

KAM conclusion
. Let us examine the sequence of operators

\left(\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\right)_{m\in\mathbb{N}}
defined by

\widehat{\Phi}_{0}:=\Phi_{0}\quad\hbox{and}\quad\quad\forall m\geqslant 1,\,\,\widehat{\Phi}_{m}:=\Phi_{0}\circ\Phi_{1}\circ...\circ\Phi_{m}.

(6.304)

It is obvious from the identity
{\Phi}_{m}=\hbox{Id}+\Psi_{m}
that
\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}=\widehat{\Phi}_{m}+\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\Psi_{m+1}
. Applying the law products yields

\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\Big(1+C\|\Psi_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\Big).

By iterating this inequality and using (
6.3.2
) we infer

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle\|\Phi_{0}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\prod_{n=1}^{m+1}\Big(1+C\|\Psi_{n}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant\displaystyle\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(1+C\,\varepsilon_{0}N_{n}^{-\mu_{2}}\Big).

Using the first condition of (
6.3.2
) and (
5.82
) imply

\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant\displaystyle\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(1+C\,\varepsilon_{0}4^{-(\frac{3}{2})^{n}}\Big)

and since the infinite product converges, we obtain for
\varepsilon_{0}
small enough

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant 2.

(6.305)

Now we shall estimate the difference
\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}
and for this aim we use the law products combined with (
6.3.2
) and (
6.305
)

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\,N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}.

(6.306)

Applying Lemma
A.1
gives

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h}).

(6.307)

Therefore, by a completeness argument we deduce that the series
\displaystyle\sum_{m\in\mathbb{N}}(\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m})
converges to an element
\Phi_{\infty}
. In addition, we get in view of (
6.3.2
) and Lemma
A.1

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}-\Phi_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{j=m}^{\infty}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{j+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{j}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\,\sum_{j=m}^{\infty}N_{j+1}^{-\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}.

(6.308)

Remark that one also finds from (
6.305
)

\|\Phi_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant 2.

(6.309)

Using (
6.307
) combined with (
6.3.2
) for
m=0
and (
6.244
)

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\infty}-\textnormal{Id}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|{\Psi}_{0}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h}).

(6.310)

Let us now check the convergence with higher order norms. Take
s\in[s_{0},S]
, then using the law products, (
6.3.2
), (
6.3.2
) and (
6.305
) we infer

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+C\|\Psi_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)+C\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

(6.311)

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+C\,\varepsilon_{0}\,N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\right)+C\,\delta_{0}^{\overline{\theta}}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}\delta_{0}^{1-\overline{\theta}}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1)N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}.

According to the first condition of (
6.3.2
) and (
5.82
) one finds

\displaystyle\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(1+C\,\varepsilon_{0}\,N_{n}^{-\mu_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leqslant\prod_{n=0}^{\infty}\Big(1+C\,\varepsilon_{0}4^{-(\frac{3}{2})^{n}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant 2,

where the last inequality holds if
\varepsilon_{0}
is chosen small enough.
Applying (
6.79
) together with (
6.311
) and Lemma
A.1
and using (
6.267
) yield

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\|{\Phi}_{0}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\delta_{0}^{\overline{\theta}}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}\delta_{0}^{1-\overline{\theta}}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1)\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(1+\delta_{0}(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})+\delta_{0}^{\overline{\theta}}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}\delta_{0}^{1-\overline{\theta}}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big).

Interpolation inequalities and (
6.3.2
) allow to get

\displaystyle\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(1+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big).

(6.312)

The next task is to estimate the difference
\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}
. By the law products combined with the first inequality in (
6.3.2
), (
6.3.2
), (
6.305
) and (
6.312
) we obtain

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\Big)

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\,N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\Big(1+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+C\,\delta^{\overline{\theta}}_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}\,\delta_{0}^{1-\overline{\theta}}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}\overline{\theta}}.

Thus, we obtain in view of Lemma
A.1

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})\Big(1+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+C\,\delta^{\overline{\theta}}_{0}(s_{h})\delta_{0}^{1-\overline{\theta}}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big).

Combining the interpolation inequalities with the second condition in (
6.3.2
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\delta_{0}(s_{h})+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big).

(6.313)

From this latter inequality combined with (
6.3.2
) and (
6.312
) we infer

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\widehat{\Phi}_{0}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(1+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big).

(6.314)

On the other hand, using (
6.313
) and the second inequality in (
6.3.2
) with
m=0,
one can check that

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\infty}-\textnormal{Id}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\|\widehat{\Phi}_{m+1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|{\Psi}_{0}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\Big(\delta_{0}(s_{h})+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big).

(6.315)

Therefore, Lemma
4.3
together with (
6.309
), (
6.3.2
) and Sobolev embeddings give

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\infty}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Phi_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\Phi_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\Big(1+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big)\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.316)

Applying (
6.181
) and (
6.284
) we obtain

\displaystyle\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)

\displaystyle=\gamma^{-1}\|\mathscr{R}_{0}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\left(1+\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right).

(6.317)

Plugging (
6.3.2
) into (
6.3.2
) and using (
6.245
) combined with Sobolev embeddings and (
6.244
) yield

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{\infty}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\left(1+\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.318)

In a similar way to (
6.3.2
) we get by Lemma
4.3
combined with (
6.3.2
) and (
6.3.2
)

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\Phi_{\infty}-\textnormal{Id}\big)\rho\big\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Phi_{\infty}-\textnormal{Id}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\Phi_{\infty}-\textnormal{Id}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{0}(s_{h})\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\Big(\delta_{0}(s_{h})+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\Big)\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta_{0}(s_{h})\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\delta_{0}\big(s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1\big)\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Hence we find from (
6.3.2
) and (
6.245
) combined with Sobolev embeddings and (
6.252
)

\displaystyle\big\|\big(\Phi_{\infty}-\textnormal{Id}\big)\rho\big\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}+\delta_{0}(s_{h})\big)\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.319)

The estimates
\Phi_{\infty}^{-1}
and
\Phi_{\infty}^{-1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}^{-1}
follow from the same type pf arguments.

➢ In what follows we plan to study the asymptotic of the eigenvalues. Summing up in
m
the estimates (
6.3.2
) and using Lemma
A.1
, we find

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\|\mu_{j}^{m+1}-\mu_{j}^{m}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\delta_{0}(s_{h}).

(6.320)

Thus for each
j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}
the sequence
(\mu_{j}^{m})_{m\in\mathbb{N}}
converges in the space
W^{q,\infty,\gamma}(\mathcal{O},\mathbb{C})
to an element denoted by
\mu_{j}^{\infty}\in W^{q,\infty,\gamma}(\mathcal{O},\mathbb{C})
.
Moreover, for any
m\in\mathbb{N},
we find in view of (
6.3.2
)

\displaystyle\|\mu_{j}^{\infty}-\mu_{j}^{m}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}\|\mu_{j}^{n+1}-\mu_{j}^{n}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\sum_{n=m}^{\infty}N_{n}^{-\mu_{2}}.

Applying Lemma
A.1

\displaystyle\sup_{j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}}\|\mu_{j}^{\infty}-\mu_{j}^{m}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}.

(6.321)

Therefore, we deduce

\displaystyle\mu_{j}^{\infty}

\displaystyle=\mu_{j}^{0}+\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\big(\mu_{j}^{m+1}-\mu_{j}^{m}\big)

\displaystyle:=\mu_{j}^{0}+r_{j}^{\infty},

(6.322)

where
(\mu_{j}^{0})
is described in Proposition
6.4
and takes the form

\mu_{j}^{0}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0})=\Omega_{j}(\lambda)+j\big(c_{i_{0}}(\lambda,\omega)-I_{1}(\lambda)K_{1}(\lambda)\big).

Hence (
6.3.2
), (
6.252
) and (
6.245
) yield

\displaystyle\|r_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\gamma\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}

and this gives the first result in (
6.248
). Define the diagonal operator
\mathscr{D}_{\infty}
defined on the normal modes by

\displaystyle\forall(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c},\quad\mathscr{D}_{\infty}{\bf e}_{l,j}={\rm i}\mu_{j}^{\infty}{\bf e}_{l,j}.

(6.323)

By the norm definition we obtain

\|\mathscr{D}_{m}-\mathscr{D}_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}=\displaystyle\sup_{j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}}\|\mu_{j}^{m}-\mu_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}},

which gives by virtue of (
6.321
)

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{D}_{m}-\mathscr{D}_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant C\,\gamma\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}.

(6.324)

➢ The next goal is to prove that the Cantor set
\mathscr{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}(i_{0})
defined in Proposition
6.5
satisfies

\mathscr{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}(i_{0})\subset\bigcap_{m=0}^{n+1}\mathscr{O}_{m}^{\gamma}=\mathscr{O}_{n+1}^{\gamma}.

where the intermediate Cantor sets are defined in (
6.281
). For this aim we shall proceed by finite induction on
m
with
n
fixed. First, we get by construction

\mathscr{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}(i_{0})\subset\mathcal{O}=:\mathscr{O}_{0}^{\gamma}.

Now assume that
\mathscr{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{2}}(i_{0})\subset\mathscr{O}_{m}^{\gamma}
for
m\leqslant n
and let us check that

\displaystyle\mathscr{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}(i_{0})\subset\mathscr{O}_{m+1}^{\gamma}.

(6.325)

Let
(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathscr{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}(i_{0})
and
(l,j,j_{0})\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times(\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c})^{2}
such that
0\leqslant|l|\leqslant N_{m}
and
(l,j)\neq(0,j_{0}).
Then, the triangle inequality, (
6.321
), (
6.244
) and (
6.3.2
) imply

\displaystyle|\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)|

\displaystyle\geqslant\displaystyle|\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega)|-2\sup_{j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}}\|\mu_{j}^{m}-\mu_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\geqslant\displaystyle\tfrac{2\gamma\langle j-j_{0}\rangle}{\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{2}}}-2\gamma\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\geqslant\displaystyle\tfrac{2\gamma\langle j-j_{0}\rangle}{\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{2}}}-2\gamma\varepsilon_{0}\langle l\rangle^{-\mu_{2}}\langle j-j_{0}\rangle.

Thus for
\varepsilon_{0}
small enough and by (
6.244
)(implying that
\mu_{2}\geqslant\tau_{2}
) we get

\big|\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)\big|>\tfrac{\gamma\langle j-j_{0}\rangle}{\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{2}}}

which shows that
(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathscr{O}_{m+1}^{\gamma}
and therefore the inclusion (
6.325
) is satisfied.

➢ Next we shall discuss the convergence of the sequence
\left(\mathscr{L}_{m}\right)_{m\in\mathbb{N}}
introduced in (
6.275
) towards the diagonal operator
\mathscr{L}_{\infty}:=\omega\cdot\partial_{\varphi}\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}+\mathscr{D}_{\infty},
where
\mathscr{D}_{\infty}
is detailed in (
6.323
). Applying (
6.324
) and (
6.285
)

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{L}_{m}-\mathscr{L}_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\mathscr{D}_{m}-\mathscr{D}_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\gamma\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}},

(6.326)

which gives in particular that

\displaystyle\lim_{m\rightarrow\infty}\|\mathscr{L}_{m}-\mathscr{L}_{\infty}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}=0.

(6.327)

By virtue of (
6.304
) and (
6.276
) one gets

\displaystyle\forall(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathscr{O}_{n+1}^{\gamma},\quad\widehat{\Phi}_{n}^{-1}\mathscr{L}_{0}\widehat{\Phi}_{n}

\displaystyle=\big(\omega\cdot\partial_{\varphi}+\mathscr{D}_{n+1}+\mathscr{R}_{n+1}\big)\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{L}_{\infty}+\big(\mathscr{D}_{n+1}-\mathscr{D}_{\infty}+\mathscr{R}_{n+1}\big)\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp},

It follows that any
(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathscr{O}_{n+1}^{\gamma}

\displaystyle\Phi_{\infty}^{-1}\mathscr{L}_{0}\Phi_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\mathscr{L}_{\infty}+\big(\mathscr{D}_{n+1}-\mathscr{D}_{\infty}+\mathscr{R}_{n+1}\big)\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle+\Phi_{\infty}^{-1}\mathscr{L}_{0}\left(\Phi_{\infty}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}\right)+\left(\Phi_{\infty}^{-1}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}^{-1}\right)\mathscr{L}_{0}\widehat{\Phi}_{n}

\displaystyle:=\mathscr{L}_{\infty}+\mathtt{E}_{n,1}^{2}+\mathtt{E}_{n,2}^{2}+\mathtt{E}_{n,3}^{2}:=\mathscr{L}_{\infty}+\mathtt{E}_{n}^{2}.

For the estimate
\mathtt{E}_{n,1}^{2}
we use (
6.324
) combined with (
6.284
),
(
6.285
), (
6.252
) and (
6.245
)

\displaystyle\|\mathtt{E}_{n,1}^{2}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\gamma\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{n+1}^{-\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{{\mu}_{2}}N_{n+1}^{-\mu_{2}}.

(6.328)

According to Lemma
4.11
with (
6.3.2
) we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathtt{E}_{n,1}^{2}\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{{\mu}_{2}}N_{n+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Now let us move to the estimates of
\mathtt{E}_{n,2}^{2}
and
\mathtt{E}_{n,3}^{2}.
They can be treated in a similar way. Therefore we shall restrict the discussion to the term
\mathtt{E}_{n,2}^{2}
. Using (
6.246
) yields

\displaystyle\|\mathtt{E}_{n,2}^{2}\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\big\|\mathscr{L}_{0}\big(\Phi_{\infty}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}\big)\rho\big\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s_{0}+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\big\|\mathscr{L}_{0}\big(\Phi_{\infty}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}\big)\rho\big\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.329)

Therefore we get from (
6.183
) combined with (
6.245
)

\displaystyle\|\mathtt{E}_{n,2}^{2}\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathscr{L}_{0}\big(\Phi_{\infty}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}\big)\rho\big\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\big(\Phi_{\infty}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}\big)\rho\big\|_{q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Applying (
6.3.2
) with Lemma
4.11
, (
6.245
) and (
6.252
) allow to get

\displaystyle\|\mathtt{E}_{n,2}^{2}\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\Phi_{\infty}-\widehat{\Phi}_{n}\big\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\delta_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\,\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Notice that for
\mathtt{E}_{n,3}^{2}
we get the same estimate as the preceding one. Consequently, putting together the foregoing estimates yields (
6.249
).

➢ The goal now is to prove (
6.248
). We set

\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s):=\max\left(\gamma^{-1}\|\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}},\gamma^{-1}\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right).

Then we shall prove by induction on
m\in\mathbb{N}
that

\displaystyle\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{0})\leqslant\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}\quad\mbox{ and }\quad\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{h})\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\right)\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h}).

(6.330)

According to Sobolev embeddings, the property is trivially satisfied for
m=0.
Notice that from (
6.181
) and (
6.245
) one gets

\displaystyle\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\left(1+\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s_{h}+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}.

(6.331)

We ssume that (
6.330
) is satisfied at the order
m
and let us check it at the order
m+1
. Applying
\partial_{\theta}
to the second identity in (
6.277
) and using (
6.286
) we obtain the expression

\displaystyle\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}

\displaystyle=\Phi_{m}^{-1}\Big(P_{N_{m}}^{\perp}\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}+\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\Psi_{m}-\Psi_{m}\partial_{\theta}\big\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\rfloor-\big[\partial_{\theta},\Psi_{m}\big]\big\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\rfloor\Big)

\displaystyle\quad+\big[\partial_{\theta},\Sigma_{m}\big]\Big(P_{N_{m}}^{\perp}\mathscr{R}_{m}+\mathscr{R}_{m}\Psi_{m}-\Psi_{m}\big\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\rfloor\Big).

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{U}_{m}^{1}+\mathcal{U}_{m}^{2}

with

\mathcal{U}_{m}^{2}=\big[\partial_{\theta},\Sigma_{m}\big]\Big(P_{N_{m}}^{\perp}\mathscr{R}_{m}+\mathscr{R}_{m}\Psi_{m}-\Psi_{m}\big\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\rfloor\Big).

It is easy to check that for any Toeplitz in time operator
T(\lambda,\omega)
, we have

\big[\partial_{\theta},T(\lambda,\omega)\big]\mathbf{e}_{l_{0},j_{0}}={\rm i}\sum_{(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d+1}}(j-j_{0})T_{j_{0}}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l-l_{0})\mathbf{e}_{l,j},

which implies using the norm definition

\big\|[\partial_{\theta},T]\big\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant\|T\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.332)

Since
\Phi_{m}^{-1}=\hbox{Id}+\Sigma_{m}
, then applying Lemma
4.3
, we obtain successively for
S\geqslant\overline{s}\geqslant s\geqslant s_{0}

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{U}_{m}^{1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left[\|\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)+\|\left[\partial_{\theta},\Psi_{m}\right]\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right]

\displaystyle+C\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left[\|\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)+\|\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right.

(6.333)

\displaystyle\left.+\|\left[\partial_{\theta},\Psi_{m}\right]\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\left[\partial_{\theta},\Psi_{m}\right]\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right]+\|P_{N_{m}}^{\perp}\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{U}_{m}^{2}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\left[\partial_{\theta},\Sigma_{m}\right]\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\|\left[\partial_{\theta},\Sigma_{m}\right]\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\left(1+\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right).

(6.334)

By using (
6.332
), (
6.267
) and Lemma
4.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\left[\partial_{\theta},\Psi_{m}\right]\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma^{-1}\|P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}\delta_{m}(s).

Coming back to (
6.287
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\left[\partial_{\theta},\Sigma_{m}\right]\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}\delta_{m}(s).

Then inserting the preceding estimates and (
6.3.2
) into (
6.3.2
) we deduce that

\displaystyle\forall\,S\geqslant\overline{s}\geqslant s\geqslant s_{0},\quad\widehat{\delta}_{m+1}(s)\leqslant N_{m}^{s-\overline{s}}\,\widehat{\delta}_{m}(\overline{s})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s)\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{0}).

(6.335)

In particular, for
s=s_{0}
we get by the induction assumption (
6.330
),

\displaystyle\widehat{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\leqslant N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{h}}\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{h})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}\left(\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{0})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\right)\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{h}}+CN_{0}^{2\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1-2\mu_{2}}\left(\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leqslant\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\left(2N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{h}}+CN_{0}^{2\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1-2\mu_{2}}\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\right).

If we fix
s_{2}
and
\mu_{2}
such that

N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{h}}\leqslant\tfrac{1}{4}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\quad\textnormal{and}\quad CN_{0}^{2\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1-2\mu_{2}}\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\leqslant\tfrac{1}{2}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}},

(6.336)

then we find

\widehat{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{0})\leqslant\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{2}}.

Notice that (
6.244
) implies in particular

s_{h}\geqslant\tfrac{3}{2}\mu_{2}+s_{0}+1\quad\textnormal{and}\quad\mu_{2}\geqslant 2(\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1)+1.

Hence, using (
5.82
), we see that the assumptions of (
6.336
) hold true provided that

4N_{0}^{-\mu_{2}}\leqslant 1\qquad\hbox{and}\qquad 2C\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\leqslant N_{0}^{-\mu_{2}}.

Remark that these conditions are satisfied thanks to (
6.290
), (
6.3.2
) and (
6.245
). Now, we turn to the proof of the second estimate in (
6.330
). By (
6.335
) and (
6.330
)

\displaystyle\widehat{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{h})

\displaystyle\leqslant\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{h})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1}\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{h})\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\right)\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\left(1+CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1-\mu_{2}}\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\right).

Taking the parameters
s_{2}
and
\mu_{2}
such that

\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+1}\right)\left(1+CN_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1-\mu_{2}}\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})\right)\leqslant 2-\tfrac{1}{m+2},

(6.337)

then we obtain

\widehat{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{h})\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+2}\right)\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h}),

which achieves the induction argument in (
6.330
). Now observe that (
6.337
) is quite similar to (
6.291
) using in particular
\mu_{2}\geqslant 2(\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2})+1
and one may proceed following the same lines. Next let us see how to get the estimate (
6.248
).
Recall that

r_{j}^{\infty}=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}r_{j}^{m}\quad\quad\textnormal{with}\quad\quad r_{j}^{m}=\big\langle P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}.

Then it is clear that

\big\langle P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1},\mathbb{C})}=\tfrac{{\rm i}}{j}\big\langle P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\partial_{\theta}\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}.

Therefore integration by parts leads to

\big\langle P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\partial_{\theta}\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1},\mathbb{C})}=-\big\langle P_{N_{m}}\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\big\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}.

Using a duality argument
H^{s_{0}}-H^{-s_{0}}
combined with Lemma
4.3
and (
6.330
), we obtain

\displaystyle\|\langle P_{N_{m}}\partial_{\theta}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\,\widehat{\delta}_{m}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\,\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}.

Putting together the preceding estimates with (
6.3.2
) and Lemma
A.1
yields

\displaystyle\|r_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\gamma\,\displaystyle|j|^{-1}\widehat{\delta}_{0}(s_{h})N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}N_{m}^{-\mu_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\displaystyle|j|^{-1}\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}.

This achieves the proof of (
6.248
).

(ii)
We shall now work with fixed values (minimal) of
\mu_{2}
and
s_{h}
denoted respectively by
\mu_{c}
and
s_{c}
, namely

\mu_{c}:=\overline{\mu_{2}}+2\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}\quad\textnormal{and}\quad s_{c}:=\frac{3}{2}\mu_{c}+\overline{s}_{l}+1=\overline{s}_{h}+4\tau_{2}q+4\tau_{2}.

(6.338)

From (
6.277
) and (
6.286
), we can write

\mathscr{R}_{m+1}=\left(\textnormal{Id}+\Sigma_{m}\right)U_{m},

where

\displaystyle U_{m}:=P_{N_{m}}^{\perp}\mathscr{R}_{m}+\mathscr{R}_{m}\Psi_{m}-\Psi_{m}\big\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\rfloor.

(6.339)

After straightforward computations, we get

\displaystyle\Delta_{12}U_{m}=

\displaystyle P_{N_{m}}^{\perp}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}+(\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m})(\Psi_{m})_{r_{1}}+(\mathscr{R}_{m})_{r_{2}}(\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m})

\displaystyle-(\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m})\big\lfloor P_{N_{m}}(\mathscr{R}_{m})_{r_{1}}\big\rfloor-(\Psi_{m})_{r_{2}}\big\lfloor P_{N_{m}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\rfloor

(6.340)

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}=\Delta_{12}U_{m}+(\Delta_{12}\Sigma_{m})(U_{m})_{r_{1}}+(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\Delta_{12}U_{m}.

(6.341)

We have used the notation
(f)_{r}=f(r).

Elementary manipulations based on (
6.286
) give

\Delta_{12}\Sigma_{m}=\Delta_{12}\Phi_{m}^{-1}=-(\Phi_{m}^{-1})_{r_{2}}(\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m})(\Phi_{m}^{-1})_{r_{1}}.

The law products of Lemma
4.3
together with (
6.303
) and (
6.245
) imply

\displaystyle\forall\,s\in[s_{0},s_{c}],\quad\|\Delta_{12}\Sigma_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.342)

Using once again the law products of Lemma
4.3
, (
6.342
) and (
6.341
) we obtain

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|(\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m})\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|(U_{m})_{r_{1}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

(6.343)

and

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|(\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m})\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|(U_{m})_{r_{1}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|(\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m})\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|(U_{m})_{r_{1}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.344)

For the estimate
(U_{m})_{r_{1}}
(to alleviate the notation we shall remove in this part remove the subscript
r_{1}
) described by (
6.339
) we use the law products leading to

\displaystyle\|U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

(6.345)

and

\displaystyle\|U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+\|\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.346)

By (
6.285
),(
6.252
) and (
6.3.2
) together with (
6.345
) we infer

\displaystyle\|U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}.

(6.347)

Putting together
the first estimate of (
6.3.2
), (
6.285
) and (
6.252
) we deduce that

\displaystyle\max_{j=1,2}\|(\Psi_{m})_{r_{j}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s_{c})

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}.

(6.348)

Hence we get in view of (
6.3.2
), (
6.285
) and (
6.245
)

\displaystyle\|U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}.

(6.349)

Plugging (
6.347
) and (
6.349
) into (
6.3.2
) implies

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+\|(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.350)

Applying (
6.287
) and (
6.252
) gives

\displaystyle\|(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\quad\hbox{and}\quad\|(\Sigma_{m})_{r_{2}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}.

(6.351)

Inserting (
6.351
) into (
6.3.2
) allows to get

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\big(1+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\big)\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.352)

In a similar way, by combining (
6.347
), (
6.351
) with (
6.3.2
) we find

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\big(1+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\big)\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.353)

From (
6.3.2
) and the law products of Lemma
4.3
we obtain
\forall\,s\in[s_{0},s_{c}],

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant

\displaystyle N_{m}^{s-s_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\max_{j=1,2}\|(\Psi_{m})_{r_{j}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\max_{j=1,2}\|(\Psi_{m})_{r_{j}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\max_{j=1,2}\|(\mathscr{R}_{m})_{r_{j}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\max_{j=1,2}\|(\mathscr{R}_{m})_{r_{j}}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Combining the foregoing estimate with (
6.3.2
), (
6.285
) and (
6.3.2
) yields

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant

\displaystyle N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

(6.354)

and

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}U_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant

\displaystyle\big(1+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\big)\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Putting together the preceding estimate with (
6.3.2
), (
6.3.2
), (
6.244
) and (
6.245
) we deduce that

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\Big(1+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{c}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\Big)\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.355)

In a similar way, by making appeal to (
6.3.2
), (
6.3.2
) and (
6.245
) we find

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m+1}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.356)

We shall now estimate
\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}.
Remark that

\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}=\sum_{\alpha\in\mathbb{N}^{d+1}\atop|\alpha|\leqslant q}\gamma^{\alpha}\sup_{(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathcal{O}}\left(\sum_{(l,k)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d+1}\atop|l|,|k|\leqslant N_{m}}\langle l,k\rangle^{2(s-|\alpha|)}\sup_{j\in\mathbb{Z}}\left|\partial_{\lambda,\omega}^{\alpha}\Delta_{12}(\Psi_{m})_{j+k}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)\right|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

By virtue of (
6.282
), we get

(\Psi_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}-(\varrho_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)r_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)&\textnormal{if }(l,j)\neq(0,j_{0})\\
0&\textnormal{if }(l,j)=(0,j_{0}),\end{array}\right.

where

(\varrho_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l):=\frac{\chi\big((\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega))(\gamma\langle j-j_{0}\rangle)^{-1}\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{2}}\big)}{\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)}\cdot

Recall from (
6.258
), that
\{{\rm i}r_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)\}
are the Fourier coefficients of
P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}
, that is

{\rm i}\,r_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)=\langle P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j_{0}},\mathbf{e}_{l,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}.

(6.357)

We can write for non-zero coefficients

\displaystyle\Delta_{12}(\Psi_{m})_{j+k}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)

\displaystyle=\Delta_{12}(\varrho_{m})_{j+k}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)\big(r_{j+k,m}^{j}\big)_{r_{1}}(\lambda,\omega,l)

\displaystyle+\big((\varrho_{m})_{j+k}^{j}\big)_{r_{2}}(\lambda,\omega,l)\Delta_{12}r_{j+k,m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l).

Hence, using Lemma
4.1
-(iv)

\displaystyle\forall q^{\prime}\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket,\quad\|\Delta_{12}(\Psi_{m})_{j+k}^{j}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Delta_{12}(\varrho_{m})_{j+k}^{j}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\max_{i\in\{1,2\}}\|\big(r_{j+k,m}^{j}\big)_{r_{i}}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+\max_{i\in\{1,2\}}\|\big((\varrho_{m})_{j+k}^{j}\big)_{r_{i}}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\Delta_{12}r_{j+k,m}^{j}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.358)

From (
6.357
), we deduce

{\rm i}\Delta_{12}r_{j_{0},m}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)=\langle P_{N_{m}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j_{0}},\mathbf{e}_{l,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}.

One can write

(\varrho_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\lambda,\omega,l)=b_{l,j,j_{0},m}\widehat{\chi}\Big(b_{l,j,j_{0},m}B_{l,j,j_{0},m}(\lambda,\omega)\Big),

with

b_{l,j,j_{0},m}:=(\gamma\langle j-j_{0}\rangle)^{-1}\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{2}},\quad B_{l,j,j_{0},m}(\lambda,\omega):=\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{m}(\lambda,\omega)-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}(\lambda,\omega),\quad\widehat{\chi}(x)=\frac{\chi(x)}{x}\cdot

Notice that from (
6.297
), one obtains

\forall q^{\prime}\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket,\quad\|B_{l,j,j_{0},m}\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\langle l,j-j_{0}\rangle.

(6.359)

In a similar way to (
6.266
), one gets from (
6.297
)

\forall q^{\prime}\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket,\quad\|(\varrho_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\gamma^{-(q^{\prime}+1)}\langle l,j-j_{0}\rangle^{\tau_{2}q^{\prime}+\tau_{2}+q^{\prime}}.

(6.360)

Using Taylor formula in a similar way to (
6.2.1
), we find (to simplify the notation we remove the dependence in
(\lambda,\omega)
)

\Delta_{12}(\varrho_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(l)=b_{l,j,j_{0},m}^{2}(\Delta_{12}B_{l,j,j_{0},m})\int_{0}^{1}\widehat{\chi}^{\prime}\Big(b_{l,j,j_{0},m}\Big[(1-\tau)(B_{l,j,_{0},m})_{r_{1}}+\tau(B_{l,j,_{0},m})_{r_{2}}\Big]\Big)d\tau.

We shall estimate
\Delta_{12}B_{l,j,j_{0},m}.
For that purpose, we use (
6.279
) to write

\mu_{j}^{m}=\mu_{j}^{0}+\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\langle P_{N_{n}}\mathscr{R}_{n}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}.

We recall from Proposition
6.4
that

\mu_{j}^{0}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0})=\Omega_{j}(\lambda)+jr^{1}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0}),\quad r^{1}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0})=c_{i_{0}}(\lambda,\omega)-V_{0}(\lambda).

Therefore

\Delta_{12}\mu_{j}^{m}=\Delta_{12}\mu_{j}^{0}+\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\langle\Delta_{12}P_{N_{n}}\mathscr{R}_{n}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{12}B_{l,j,j_{0},m}

\displaystyle=\Delta_{12}\big(\mu_{j}^{m}-\mu_{j_{0}}^{m}\big)

\displaystyle=(j-j_{0})\Delta_{12}c_{i}+\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\langle\Delta_{12}P_{N_{n}}\mathscr{R}_{n}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\langle\Delta_{12}P_{N_{n}}\mathscr{R}_{n}\mathbf{e}_{0,j_{0}},\mathbf{e}_{0,j_{0}}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}.

Hence, using (
6.30
), one gets

\forall q^{\prime}\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket,\quad\|\Delta_{12}B_{l,j,j_{0},m}\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\varepsilon|j-j_{0}|\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q^{\prime},\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\|P_{N_{n}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{n}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q^{\prime},s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.361)

Then, one obtains from Lemma
4.1
-(vi), (
6.359
) and (
6.361
)

\displaystyle\forall q^{\prime}\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket,\quad\|\Delta_{12}(\varrho_{m})_{j_{0}}^{j}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2-q^{\prime}}\langle l,j-j_{0}\rangle^{\tau_{2}q^{\prime}+2\tau_{2}+q^{\prime}+1}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q^{\prime},\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

(6.362)

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma^{-2-q^{\prime}}\langle l,j-j_{0}\rangle^{\tau_{2}q^{\prime}+2\tau_{2}+q^{\prime}}\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\|P_{N_{n}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{n}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q^{\prime},s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Gathering (
6.3.2
), (
6.360
) and (
6.362
) gives for all
q^{\prime}\in\llbracket 0,q\rrbracket
,

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}(\Psi_{m})_{j+k}^{j}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2-q^{\prime}}\langle l,k\rangle^{\tau_{2}q^{\prime}+2\tau_{2}+q^{\prime}+1}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q^{\prime},\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\max_{i\in\{1,2\}}\|\big(r_{j+k,m}^{j}\big)_{r_{i}}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+\gamma^{-2-q^{\prime}}\langle l,k\rangle^{\tau_{2}q^{\prime}+2\tau_{2}+q^{\prime}}\max_{i\in\{1,2\}}\|\big(r_{j+k,m}^{j}\big)_{r_{i}}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\|P_{N_{n}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{n}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q^{\prime},s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle+\gamma^{-1-q^{\prime}}\langle l,k\rangle^{\tau_{2}q^{\prime}+\tau_{2}+q^{\prime}}\|\Delta_{12}r_{j+k,m}^{j}(\ast,l)\|_{q^{\prime}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

We deduce that for all
s\in[s_{0},S],

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2-q}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma^{-2-q}\|P_{N_{m}}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\|P_{N_{n}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{n}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma^{-1-q}\|P_{N_{m}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.363)

We set

\overline{\delta}_{m}(s)=\gamma^{-1}\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\quad\textnormal{and}\quad\varkappa_{m}(s):=\sum_{n=0}^{m-1}\overline{\delta}_{n}(s).

(6.364)

Then, using (
6.3.2
), (
6.284
) and (
6.3
), we get

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-1-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\delta_{m}(\overline{s}_{l})+\gamma^{-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(\overline{s}_{l})\varkappa_{m}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma^{-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{0})

(6.365)

and

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-1-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}+1}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\delta_{m}(s_{c})+\gamma^{-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}}\delta_{m}(s_{c})\varkappa_{m}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma^{-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{c}).

(6.366)

According to (
6.3.2
), one has

\delta_{m}(\overline{s}_{l})\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}\quad\textnormal{and}\quad\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\delta_{m}(s_{c})\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\lesssim 1.

(6.367)

Putting together (
6.367
) and (
6.3.2
) and using (
6.245
) yields

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+N_{m}^{\tau_{2}-\mu_{c}}\varkappa_{m}(s_{0})+\gamma^{-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{0}).

(6.368)

In a similar way, on gets by (
6.367
), (
6.3.2
) and (
6.245
)

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}\Psi_{m}\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{c}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}+1}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}}\varkappa_{m}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+\gamma^{-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{c}).

(6.369)

Plugging (
6.368
) into (
6.3.2
) yields by virtue of (
6.285
) and (
6.245
)

\displaystyle\overline{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\leqslant N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{c}}\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{c})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{c}}\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{0})+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}-2\mu_{c}}\varkappa_{m}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}-2\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.370)

Therefore, inserting (
6.368
), (
6.3.2
) into (
6.3.2
) and using (
6.245
) implies

\displaystyle\overline{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{c})

\displaystyle\leqslant\Big(1+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{c}}+CN_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{c}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\Big)\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{c})+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-2-q}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\overline{\delta}_{m}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\quad+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{c}}\varkappa_{m}(s_{0})+C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}+1-\mu_{c}}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.371)

Next, we intend to prove by induction in
m\in\mathbb{N}
that

\forall\,k\leqslant m,\quad\overline{\delta}_{k}(s_{0})\leqslant N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{k}^{-\mu_{c}}\nu(s_{c})\quad\mbox{ and }\quad\overline{\delta}_{k}(s_{c})\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{k+1}\right)\nu(s_{c}),

(6.372)

with

\nu(s):=\overline{\delta}_{0}(s)+\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+2}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

The estimate (
6.372
) is obvious for
m=0
by Sobolev embeddings. Now let us
assume that the preceding property holds true at the order
m
and let us check it at the order
m+1
. Thus by applying (
6.364
) and Lemma
A.1
, we get

\sup_{m\in\mathbb{N}}\varkappa_{m}(s_{0})\leqslant C\nu(s_{c}).

Putting together this estimate with the induction assumption, (
6.3.2
), (
6.3.2
),(
6.338
) and (
6.245
) yields

\displaystyle\overline{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\leqslant\left(2N_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{c}}+CN_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-2\mu_{c}}\right)\nu(s_{c})

and

\displaystyle\overline{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{c})

\displaystyle\leqslant\Big(1+CN_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{c}}+CN_{m}^{s_{0}-s_{c}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}}\Big)\left(2-\frac{1}{m+1}\right)\nu(s_{c})

\displaystyle\quad+C\left(N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}-\mu_{c}}+N_{m}^{\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}+1-\mu_{c}}\right)\nu(s_{c}).

Since (
6.338
) implies in particular

\mu_{c}\geqslant 2\tau_{2}q+2\tau_{2}+1\quad\textnormal{and}\quad s_{c}\geqslant\tfrac{3}{2}\mu_{c}+s_{0}+\tau_{2}q+\tau_{2}+1,

then proceeding similarly to the proof of (
6.285
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\overline{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{0})

\displaystyle\leqslant N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m+1}^{-\mu_{c}}\nu(s_{c})\quad\hbox{and}\quad\overline{\delta}_{m+1}(s_{c})\leqslant\left(2-\tfrac{1}{m+2}\right)\nu(s_{c}),

which achieves the induction. The next target is to estimate
\Delta_{12}r_{j}^{\infty}
. Then similarly to (
6.3.2
) we obtain through
a duality argument, Lemma
4.3
, (
6.372
) and Lemma
A.1

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}r_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\Big\|\langle P_{N_{m}}\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\mathbf{e}_{0,j},\mathbf{e}_{0,j}\rangle_{L^{2}(\mathbb{T}^{d+1})}\Big\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\big\|\Delta_{12}\mathscr{R}_{m}\big\|_{\textnormal{\tiny{O-d}},q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\gamma\nu(s_{c})\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}N_{0}^{\mu_{c}}N_{m}^{-\mu_{c}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\,\nu(s_{c}).

From the particular value of
\mathtt{p}
in (
6.177
), we infer

s_{c}=\overline{s}_{h}+4\tau_{2}q+4\tau_{2}=\overline{s}_{h}+\mathtt{p}.

(6.373)

Then, applying (
6.182
) we obtain

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{12}r_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\leqslant C\gamma\,\nu(\overline{s}_{h}+4\tau_{2}q+4\tau_{2})

\displaystyle\leqslant C\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}\,\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Finally, combining the previous estimate with (
6.3.2
) and (
6.179
) we deduce

\displaystyle\forall j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c},\quad\|\Delta_{12}\mu_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Delta_{12}\mu_{j}^{0}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\Delta_{12}r_{j}^{\infty}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\varepsilon\gamma^{-1}|j|\|\Delta_{12}i\|_{q,\overline{s}_{h}+\sigma_{4}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

The achieves the proof of Proposition
6.5
.
∎

6.4
Approximate inverse in the normal directions

In this section we plan to construct an approximate right inverse in the normal directions for the linearized operator
\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{\omega}
defined in (
5.87
) when the parameters are restricted in a Cantor like set. Our main result is the following.

Proposition 6.6
.

Let
(\gamma,q,d,\tau_{1},s_{0},S)
satisfying (
4.1
) and (
4.2
). There exists
\sigma:=\sigma(\tau_{1},\tau_{2},q,d)\geqslant\sigma_{4}
such that if

\varepsilon\gamma^{-2-q}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}\leqslant\varepsilon_{0}\quad\textnormal{and}\quad\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s_{h}+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant 1,

(6.374)

then the following assertions hold true.

(i)

Consider the operator
\mathscr{L}_{\infty}
defined in Proposition
\ref{reduction of the remainder term},
then there exists a family of linear operators
\big(\mathtt{T}_{n}\big)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
defined in
\mathcal{O}
satisfying the estimate

\forall s\in[s_{0},S],\quad\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\|\mathtt{T}_{n}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\gamma^{-1}\|\rho\|_{s+\tau_{1}q+\tau_{1}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

and such that for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
, in the Cantor set

\Lambda_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1}}(i_{0})=\bigcap_{(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}\atop|l|\leqslant N_{n}}\left\{(\lambda,\omega)\in\mathcal{O}\quad\textnormal{s.t.}\quad\left|\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0})\right|>\tfrac{\gamma\langle j\rangle}{\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{1}}}\right\},

we have

\mathscr{L}_{\infty}\mathtt{T}_{n}=\textnormal{Id}+\mathtt{E}^{3}_{n},

with

\forall s_{0}\leqslant s\leqslant\overline{s}\leqslant S,\quad\|\mathtt{E}^{3}_{n}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim N_{n}^{s-\overline{s}}\gamma^{-1}\|\rho\|_{\overline{s}+1+\tau_{1}q+\tau_{1}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(ii)

There exists a family of linear operators
\big(\mathtt{T}_{\omega,n}\big)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
satisfying

\forall\,s\in\,[s_{0},S],\quad\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\|\mathtt{T}_{\omega,n}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\gamma^{-1}\left(\|\rho\|_{q,s+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)

(6.375)

and such that in the Cantor set

\mathcal{G}_{n}(\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2},i_{0}):=\mathcal{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1}}(i_{0})\cap\mathcal{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}(i_{0})\cap\Lambda_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1}}(i_{0}),

we have

\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{\omega}\mathtt{T}_{\omega,n}=\textnormal{Id}+\mathtt{E}_{n},

where
\mathtt{E}_{n}
satisfies the following estimate

\displaystyle\forall\,s\in[s_{0},S],\quad

\displaystyle\|\mathtt{E}_{n}\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim N_{n}^{s_{0}-s}\gamma^{-1}\Big(\|\rho\|_{q,s+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\Big)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\varepsilon\gamma^{-3}N_{0}^{{\mu}_{2}}N_{n+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Recall that
\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{\omega},

\mathcal{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1}}(i_{0})
and
\mathcal{O}_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2}}(i_{0})
are given in Propositions
\ref{lemma setting for Lomega},\ref{reduction of the transport part}
and
\ref{reduction of the remainder term},
respectively.

(iii)

In the Cantor set
\mathcal{G}_{n}(\gamma,\tau_{1},\tau_{2},i_{0})
, we have the following splitting

\widehat{\mathcal{L}}_{\omega}=\widehat{\mathtt{L}}_{\omega,n}+\widehat{\mathtt{R}}_{n}\quad\textnormal{with}\quad\widehat{\mathtt{L}}_{\omega,n}\mathtt{T}_{\omega,n}=\textnormal{Id}\quad\textnormal{and}\quad\widehat{\mathtt{R}}_{n}=\mathtt{E}_{n}\widehat{\mathtt{L}}_{\omega,n},

where the operators
\widehat{\mathtt{L}}_{\omega,n}
and
\widehat{\mathtt{R}}_{n}
are defined in
\mathcal{O}
and satisfy the following estimates

\displaystyle\forall s\in[s_{0},S],\quad

\displaystyle\sup_{n\in\mathbb{N}}\|\widehat{\mathtt{L}}_{\omega,n}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\|\rho\|_{q,s+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}},

\displaystyle\forall s\in[s_{0},S],\quad

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathtt{R}}_{n}\rho\|_{q,s_{0}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim N_{n}^{s_{0}-s}\gamma^{-1}\left(\|\rho\|_{q,s+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}+\varepsilon\gamma^{-2}\|\mathfrak{I}_{0}\|_{q,s+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\varepsilon\gamma^{-3}N_{0}^{\mu_{2}}N_{n+1}^{-\mu_{2}}\|\rho\|_{q,s_{0}+\sigma}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Proof.

(i)
From Proposition
6.5
we recall that

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\omega\cdot\partial_{\varphi}\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}+\mathscr{D}_{\infty}.

Then we may split this operator as follows, using the projectors defined in (
4.4
)

\displaystyle\mathscr{L}_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\Pi_{N_{n}}\omega\cdot\partial_{\varphi}\Pi_{N_{n}}\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}+\mathscr{D}_{\infty}-\Pi_{N_{n}}^{\perp}\omega\cdot\partial_{\varphi}\Pi_{N_{n}}^{\perp}\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}

\displaystyle:=\mathtt{L}_{n}-\mathtt{R}_{n},

(6.376)

with
\mathtt{R}_{n}:=\Pi_{N_{n}}^{\perp}\omega\cdot\partial_{\varphi}\Pi_{N_{n}}^{\perp}\Pi_{\mathbb{S}_{0}}^{\perp}.
From this definition and the structure of
\mathscr{D}_{\infty}
in Proposition
6.5
we deduce that

\forall(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c},\quad{\bf e}_{-l,-j}\mathtt{L}_{n}{\bf e}_{l,j}=\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}{\rm i}\big(\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{\infty}\big)&\hbox{if }|l|\leqslant N_{n}\\
{\rm i}\,\mu_{j}^{\infty}&\hbox{if }|l|>N_{n}.\end{array}\right.

Define the diagonal operator
\mathtt{T}_{n}
by

\displaystyle\mathtt{T}_{n}\rho(\lambda,\omega,\varphi,\theta):=

\displaystyle-{\rm i}\sum_{(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}\atop|l|\leqslant N_{n}}\tfrac{\chi\left((\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0}))\gamma^{-1}\langle l\rangle^{\tau_{1}}\right)}{\omega\cdot l+\mu_{j}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0})}\rho_{l,j}(\lambda,\omega)\,e^{{\rm i}(l\cdot\varphi+j\theta)}

\displaystyle-{\rm i}\sum_{(l,j)\in\mathbb{Z}^{d}\times\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c}\atop|l|>N_{n}}\tfrac{\rho_{l,j}(\lambda,\omega)}{\mu_{j}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0})}\,e^{{\rm i}(l\cdot\varphi+j\theta)},

where
\chi
is the cut-off function defined in (
5.81
) and
\left(\rho_{l,j}(\lambda,\omega)\right)_{l,j}
are the Fourier coefficients of
\rho
. We recall from Proposition
6.5
that

\mu_{j}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega,i_{0})=\Omega_{j}(\lambda)+jr^{1}(\lambda,\omega)+r_{j}^{\infty}(\lambda,\omega)\quad\mbox{ with }\quad r^{1}(\lambda,\omega)=c_{i_{0}}(\lambda,\omega)-V_{0}(\lambda),

with the estimates

\forall j\in\mathbb{S}_{0}^{c},\quad\|\mu^{\infty}_{j}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim|j|,

where we use in part the estimate (
6.274
), (
6.248
) and (
6.179
). According to Lemma
3.3
-(iii), (
6.274
), (
6.248
) and the smallness condition (
6.245
) we infer

|j|\lesssim\|\mu^{\infty}_{j}\|_{0}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\leqslant\|\mu^{\infty}_{j}\|_{q}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}},

Implementing the same arguments as for (
6.267
) one gets

\displaystyle\forall s\geqslant s_{0},\quad\|\mathtt{T}_{n}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim\gamma^{-1}\|\rho\|_{s+\tau_{1}q+\tau_{1}}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

(6.377)

Moreover, by construction

\mathtt{L}_{n}\mathtt{T}_{n}=\textnormal{Id}\quad\textnormal{in }\Lambda_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1}}(i_{0})

(6.378)

since
\chi(\cdot)=1
in this set. It follows from (
6.4
) that

\displaystyle\forall\,(\lambda,\omega)\in\Lambda_{\infty,n}^{\gamma,\tau_{1}}(i_{0}),\quad\mathscr{L}_{\infty}\mathtt{T}_{n}

\displaystyle=\textnormal{Id}-\mathtt{R}_{n}\mathtt{T}_{n}

\displaystyle:=\textnormal{Id}+\mathtt{E}_{n}^{3}.

(6.379)

Notice that by Lemma
4.1
-(ii),

\forall\,s_{0}\leqslant s\leqslant\overline{s},\quad\|\mathtt{R}_{n}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}\lesssim N_{n}^{s-\overline{s}}\|\rho\|_{\overline{s}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}.

Combining this estimate with (
6.377
) yields

\displaystyle\forall\,s_{0}\leqslant s\leqslant\overline{s},\quad\|\mathtt{E}^{3}_{n}\rho\|_{q,s}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}

\displaystyle\lesssim N_{n}^{s-\overline{s}}\|\mathtt{T}_{n}\rho\|_{\overline{s}+1}^{\gamma,\mathcal{O}}
